  THE UNIT IS NOT THE EXPLANATION
    speedup in sequence items : 20.70x
    speedup in TX_DATA pushes : 21.08x
    they differ by            : 1.02x -- so the unit was
    unlabelled, which is worth fixing, and is not the cause.

  WHAT IS: see summarize_closure.py over the accumulated
  seeds.  In one sentence -- the STEERED closure cost
  reproduces across language, framework and RNG, and the
  PURE-RANDOM one does not, so 13.68x is a ratio with a
  stable denominator and an unstable numerator.
